----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : KALIP / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : WAC_PRCLST
Hangi Tablo için : STOK48
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : WAC Fiyat listenin aktarýmý
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
    
    MaliyetTuru = "WC" 'Envanterdeki hammadde maliyeti
    MaliyetAdi = "WAC sistemyle hesaplanan YTL stok maliyeti"
    MyDateStr = InputBox("Maliyet tarihini giriniz", "Maliyet tarihi", Date)
    MyDate = CDate(MyDateStr)
    MyPriceUnit = "YTL"

    If Trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("STMTYP", "KOD", MaliyetTuru, d7c_ErrMsgNo)) = "" Then
        ret = GrupKoduYarat("STMTYP", MaliyetTuru, MaliyetAdi)
    End If
    If ret <> 0 Then
        k = Doc.d7MsgBox(MaliyetTuru & " Maliyet endeksi yaratýlamadý. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.", "dinamo", MB_OK)
        Exit Sub
    End If

    Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("STDMH0", "")
    Set STDMH0E = Doc1.GetTableObject("STDMH0E")
    Set STDMH0T = Doc1.GetTableObject("STDMH0T")
    For i = 1 To STOK48T.GetRecCount()
        If Trim(STOK48T.KOD(i)) <> "" Then
            
            StokKodu = STOK48T.KOD(i)
            MyTutar = STOK48T.PRICE(i)
            
            VarMi = StandartMaliyetdeVarmi(StokKodu,MaliyetTuru)

					if VarMi = "" then
                ret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
                STDMH0E.STDMALTYPE = MaliyetTuru
                STDMH0E.KOD = StokKodu
                STDMH0T.AddRow
                DetaySatirNo = STDMH0T.GetRecCount
            Else
            		Rows = Doc.SelectEQ("STDMH0E", "KOD", StokKodu)
                Set ST = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_STDMH0E_SELECT")
                For j = 1 To ST.GetRecCount()
                    If Trim(ST.STDMALTYPE(j)) = MaliyetTuru Then
                        EvrakNo = ST.EvrakNo(j)
                    End If
                Next
                If Trim(EvrakNo) <> "" Then
                    ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(EvrakNo)
                    If ret <> 0 Then
                        k = Doc.d7MsgBox(Trim(StokKodu) & ": Standart Maliyet tablosu yüklenemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.", "dinamo", MB_OK)
                        Exit Sub
                    End If
                    STDMH0T.AddRow
                    DetaySatirNo = STDMH0T.GetRecCount
                Else
                    ret = Doc1.New_Voucher()
                    STDMH0E.STDMALTYPE = MaliyetTuru
                    STDMH0E.KOD = StokKodu
                    STDMH0T.AddRow
                    DetaySatirNo = STDMH0T.GetRecCount
                End If
            End If
            STDMH0T.VALIDAFTERTARIH(DetaySatirNo) = Doc.StrToNorDate(MyDate)
            STDMH0T.Unsur(DetaySatirNo) = "HM"
            STDMH0T.TUTAR(DetaySatirNo) = MyTutar
            STDMH0T.PARABIRIMI(DetaySatirNo) = MyPriceUnit

            ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
            If ret <> 0 Then
                k = Doc.d7MsgBox(Trim(StokKodu) & ": Standart Maliyet tablosu kaydedilemedi. Ýþlem kesilmiþtir.", "dinamo", MB_OK)
                ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
                Exit Sub
            End If
            ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
        End If
    Next
End Sub

Function GrupKoduYarat(GrupKoduTipi, GrupKodu, GrupAciklamasi)
    Set Doc1 = Doc.CreateDocumentNoUI("GECO10", "")
    Set GT = Doc1.GetTableObject("GECOUST")
    ret = Doc1.Load_Voucher(GrupKoduTipi)
    If ret <> 0 Then
        GrupKoduYarat = 1
        ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
        Exit Function
    End If
    GT.AddRow
    satirNo = GT.GetRecCount
    GT.KOD(satirNo) = GrupKodu
    GT.AD(satirNo) = GrupAciklamasi
    ret = Doc1.Save_Voucher()
    If ret <> 0 Then
        GrupKoduYarat = 1
    Else
        GrupKoduYarat = 0
    End If
    ret = Doc1.LoadEmpty_Voucher()
End Function

Function satirBul(STDMH0T, Unsur)
    satirBul = 0
    For i = 1 To STDMH0T.GetRowCount()
        If STDMH0T.VALIDAFTERTARIH(i) + STDMH0T.Unsur(i) = Doc.BuGun + Unsur Then
            satirBul = i
            Exit Function
        End If
    Next
End Function

Function StandartMaliyetdeVarmi(StokKodu,MaliyetTuru)
	StandartMaliyetdeVarmi=""
	Dim Kriter
	Kriter = "KOD=" & StokKodu
	Kriter = Kriter & "&"
 	Kriter = Kriter & "STDMALTYPE=" & MaliyetTuru
	Dim Tbl
	Set Tbl=Doc.Select1("Tbl_53554534","STDMH0E",Kriter,"",-1)
	If Tbl.GetRowCount>=1 Then
		StandartMaliyetdeVarmi=Tbl.Evrakno(1)
	End If
End Function

Sub Makro2()

				ymamul_miktar = trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("MMPS10T","R_YMAMULMIKTAR", SFDC31E.JOBNO, "JOBNO"))
				ymamul_sure = trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("MMPS10T","R_MIKTAR0", SFDC31E.JOBNO, "JOBNO")) * 60
				sure = doc.Div(ymamul_sure, ymamul_miktar)
				msgbox sure

End Sub
Sub Makro1()
       nRows2 = doc.SelectEQ("MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO", SFDC31E.MPSNO)
        Set nMMPS2 = doc.GetTableObject("AU_MMPS10T_SELECT")
        For ni2 = 1 To nMMPS2.GetRecCount()
            If Trim(nMMPS2.jobno(ni2)) = Trim(SFDC31E.jobno) Then
                ymamul_miktar = Trim(nMMPS2.R_YMAMULMIKTAR(ni2))
                ymamul_sure = Trim(nMMPS2.R_MIKTAR0(ni2)) * 60
                sure = doc.Div(ymamul_sure, ymamul_miktar)
            End If
        Next
				msgbox sure
End Sub

Sub Makro1()
dim sayi
sayi=0
k=doc.d7msgbox("Listelediðiniz HESAPLAR silinecek.Emin misiniz?","DÝKKAT",MB_YESNO)
	if k=6 then
			for i=1 to LISTET.GetReccount()
				Doc.StatusbarMessage "Siliniyor : " & i & " / " & LISTET.GetRecCount()
				evrakno=LISTET.EVRAKNO(i)
				nret=doc.load_voucher(evrakno)
				nret=Doc.Delete_Voucher()
				sayi=sayi+1
			next
				Msgbox ("Silme iþlemi tamamlandý.Toplam "& sayi &" adet MPS silindi.")
		else
			exit sub
  end if
End Sub

----------------------------------------------------------------
Makro Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : KALIP / Uygulama : MAKR7S
Makro Kodu : proje
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : PROJE YAYINLAMA-ORG-XXXX
Kod : 
Sub Makro1()
	
	Set MPS10T=Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_BAGLANTILI_LISTE")
	Set MPS10E=Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E_BAGLANTILI_LISTE")
	Set MPS10TM=Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")
	Set MPS10L=Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E")	

	RecE=MPS10E.GetRecCount()

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Add	
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets.Add

'******** Excel Sutun Baþlýklarý ****** 

	Sh1.Cells(1,1)="MÜÞTERÝ:"& trim(MPS10TM.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(1))
	Sh1.Cells(2,1)="PROJE KODU:"&trim(MPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(1))
	Sh1.Cells(1,2)="Süre(Gün)"
	Sh1.Cells(1,3)="Baþlangýç tarihi"
	Sh1.Cells(1,4)="Bitiþ Tarihi"
	Sh1.Cells(1,5)="Süre(saat)"
	Sh1.Cells(1,6)="Tezgah"
	Sh1.Cells(1,7)="Statü"
	Sh1.Cells(1,8)="Bitti mi?"
	Sh1.Cells(1,9)="Baþlama Saati"
	Sh1.Cells(1,10)="Bitiþ Saati"
	Sh1.Columns("K:IV").columnwidth=8
'	Sh1.Range("k1:M1").merge
'	Sh1.Range("k1:m1").value="Günler"
	Sh1.Range("A1:IV250").font.size=8
	Sh1.Range("A1:A100").RowHeight=9.75	

satir=1
kolon=1

enb="0000/01/01"
enk="2999/12/31"


'****  MPS Bilgileri okunuyor *****

	for i=2 to RecE
			satir=satir+2
			MPS10E.SetCurrentRow(i)
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon).value=trim(MPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(i))
			sh1.Cells(satir,kolon).interior.colorindex=	17
			Doc.RunCommand("CMD_ANALIZ_SONUCLARI")

		rows=MPS10T.GetRowCount()

		for k=1 to rows
			satir=satir+1
			sure=Doc.CDateToint(MPS10T.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(k))-Doc.Cdatetoint(MPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(k))
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon).value=trim(MPS10T.R_OPERASYON_IMLT01_AD(k))
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon).interior.colorindex=36
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+1).numberformat="0"
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+1).value=sure
			Sh1.Cells(satir,kolon+2).numberformat="@"
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+2).value=Doc.StrToNorDate(MPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(k))
			Sh1.Cells(satir,kolon+3).numberformat="@"
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+3).value=Doc.StrToNorDate(MPS10T.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(k))
			sh1.Cells(satir,kolon+4).value=MPS10T.R_MIKTART(k)
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+5).value=trim(MPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU_IMLT00_AD(k))
  			sh1.Cells(satir,kolon+8).value=MPS10T.R_LATEST_B_SAAT(k)
			sh1.Cells(satir,kolon+9).value=MPS10T.R_LATEST_E_SAAT(k)
			sh1.Cells(satir,kolon+10).value=MPS10T.JOBNO(k)

			statu=MPS10E.STATUS(i)
			
			Doc.StatusBarMessage "Proje planý yayýn için hazýrlanýyor.Lütfen bekleyin...!"			

' En büyük ve en küçük tarih deðerleri alýnýyor

			if enk> MPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(k) then
				enk=MPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(k)
			end if

			if enb< MPS10T.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(k) then
				enb=MPS10T.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(k)
			end if

	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("SFDC20T","JOBNO",trim(MPS10T.JOBNO(k)))
	set atolye = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_SFDC20T_SELECT")
		For z=1 to atolye.GetRowCount()
			if enk> atolye.RECTARIH1(z) then
				enk=atolye.RECTARIH1(z)
			end if

			if enb< atolye.RECTARIH1(z) then
				enb=atolye.RECTARIH1(z)
			end if

			if enk> atolye.RECTARIH2(z) then
				enk=atolye.RECTARIH2(z)
			end if

			if enb< atolye.RECTARIH2(z) then
				enb=atolye.RECTARIH2(z)
			end if
	Next

			enk1=Doc.CDateToint(enk)
			enb1=Doc.CDateToint(enb)

'Alýnan tarih deðerleri sütunlara yazdýrýlýyor			
		n=11
		for ttt=enk1 to enb1
			n=n+1
			Sh1.Cells(3,n).numberformat="@"
			sh1.Cells(3,n).value=Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.Date_NumToStr(ttt))
			sh1.Cells(3,n).Font.Size=8
		next

'iþin statüsüne göre hücreler boyanýyor.

		if statu="P" then
			statu="PLANLANMIÞ"
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+6).value=statu
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+6).interior.colorindex=41 
		elseif statu="R" then
			statu="BAÞLAMIÞ"
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+6).value=statu
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+6).interior.colorindex=3
		elseif statu="C" then
			statu="KESÝNLEÞMÝÞ"
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+6).value=statu
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+6).interior.colorindex=4
		end if

		if MPS10T.R_ACIK_KAPALI(k)="K" then
			durum=MPS10T.R_ACIK_KAPALI(k)
			durum="Evet"
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+7).value=durum
				if durum="Evet" then
	 				Sh1.Cells(satir,7).interior.colorindex=16
					Sh1.Cells(satir,7).value="Tamamlandý"		
					sh1.cells(satir,7).font.colorindex=2
				end if	
		end if
	next 
next 


' Ýþin tamamlanma zamanýna göre (sure sütunu)hücreler  renklendirliyor.

for jj=4 to satir
 for kk=12 to n
		if sh1.cells(jj,3).value=sh1.cells(3,kk).value and sh1.Cells(jj,7).value="BAÞLAMIÞ" then
			sh1.cells(jj,kk).interior.colorindex=3
				for sss=1 to sh1.cells(jj,2).value
					sh1.cells(jj,kk+sss).interior.colorindex=3
				next
		elseif sh1.cells(jj,3).value=sh1.cells(3,kk).value and sh1.Cells(jj,7).value="Tamamlandý"then
				sh1.cells(jj,kk).interior.colorindex=16
				for sss=1 to sh1.cells(jj,2).value
					sh1.cells(jj,kk+sss).interior.colorindex=16
				next
		elseif sh1.cells(jj,3).value=sh1.cells(3,kk).value and sh1.Cells(jj,7).value="PLANLANMIÞ"then
			sh1.cells(jj,kk).interior.colorindex=41
				for sss=1 to sh1.cells(jj,2).value
					sh1.cells(jj,kk+sss).interior.colorindex=41
				next
		elseif sh1.cells(jj,3).value=sh1.cells(3,kk).value and sh1.Cells(jj,7).value="KESÝNLEÞMÝÞ"then
			sh1.cells(jj,kk).interior.colorindex=4
				for sss=1 to sh1.cells(jj,2).value
					sh1.cells(jj,kk+sss).interior.colorindex=4
				next
		 end if

rows2 = Doc.SelectEQ("SFDC20T","JOBNO",trim(Sh1.Cells(jj,11).value))
set atolye = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_SFDC20T_SELECT")
For z=1 to atolye.GetRowCount()
		if	(Doc.StrToNorDate(atolye.RECTARIH1(z)) = Sh1.Cells(3,kk).Value) then
					sh1.Cells(jj,kk).Value="XXXXXXX"
				for sss=1 to (Doc.CDateToint(atolye.RECTARIH2(z))-Doc.CDateToint(atolye.RECTARIH1(z)))	'EGER TARÝH DEVAM EDÝYORSA DÝGER GÜNLERÝDE BOYA
					sh1.cells(jj,kk).interior.colorindex=4
					sh1.Cells(jj,kk).Value="XXXXXXX"
				next
		 end if
Next

	next
next

Excel.Visible=true

End Sub

Sub Makro1()

	Set MPS10T=Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10T_BAGLANTILI_LISTE")
	Set MPS10E=Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10E_BAGLANTILI_LISTE")
	Set MPS10TM=Doc.GetTableObject("MMPS10TM")

	RecE=MPS10E.GetRecCount()

	Set Excel = CreateObject("Excel.Application")
	Set Wb1 = Excel.Workbooks.Add	
	Set Sh1 = Wb1.Sheets.Add

	Sh1.Cells(1,1)="MÜÞTERÝ:"& trim(MPS10TM.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(1))
	Sh1.Cells(2,1)="PROJE KODU:"&trim(MPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(1))
	Sh1.Cells(1,2)="Süre(Gün)"
	Sh1.Cells(1,3)="Baþlangýç tarihi"
	Sh1.Cells(1,4)="Bitiþ Tarihi"
	Sh1.Cells(1,5)="Süre(saat)"
	Sh1.Cells(1,6)="Tezgah"
	Sh1.Cells(1,7)="Statü"
	Sh1.Cells(1,8)="Bitti mi?"
	Sh1.Cells(1,9)="Baþlama Saati"
	Sh1.Cells(1,10)="Bitiþ Saati"
	Sh1.Columns("K:WM").columnwidth=4.4
'	Sh1.Cells(1,11)="Gün"
	Sh1.Range("k1:M1").merge
	Sh1.Range("k1:m1").value="Günler"
	
 
satir=1
kolon=1

	for i=2 to RecE
			satir=satir+1
			MPS10E.SetCurrentRow(i)
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon).value=trim(MPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(i))
			sh1.Cells(satir,kolon).interior.colorindex=	17
			Doc.RunCommand("CMD_ANALIZ_SONUCLARI")
			rows=MPS10T.GetRowCount()
		for k=1 to rows
			satir=satir+2
			sure=Doc.CDateToint(MPS10T.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(k))-Doc.Cdatetoint(MPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(k))
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon).value=trim(MPS10T.R_OPERASYON_IMLT01_AD(k))
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon).interior.colorindex=36
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+1).value=sure
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+2).value=MPS10T.R_LATEST_B_TARIH(k)
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+3).value=MPS10T.R_LATEST_E_TARIH(k)
			sh1.Cells(satir,kolon+4).value=MPS10T.R_MIKTART(k)
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+5).value=trim(MPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU_IMLT00_AD(k))
  			sh1.Cells(satir,kolon+8).value=MPS10T.R_LATEST_B_SAAT(k)
			sh1.Cells(satir,kolon+9).value=MPS10T.R_LATEST_E_SAAT(k)

			Call gunyaz(sh1,sure)
		
		if MPS10T.R_ACIK_KAPALI(k)="K" then
			durum=MPS10T.R_ACIK_KAPALI(k)
			durum="Evet"
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+7).value=durum
				if durum="Evet" and sure>=0 then
	 				Sh1.Cells(satir,11).interior.colorindex=1
				end if	
		end if
		
statu=MPS10E.STATUS(i)

		if statu="P" then
			statu="PLANLANMIÞ"
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+6).value=statu
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+6).interior.colorindex=41 
			Call HucreRenk(sh1,sure,satir,41)
		elseif statu="R" and sure>0 then
			statu="BAÞLAMIÞ"
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+6).value=statu
			sh1.cells(satir,kolon+6).interior.colorindex=3
			Call HucreRenk(sh1,sure,satir,46)
		end if
	next 
next   
Excel.Visible=true
End Sub

Sub HucreRenk(sh1,ss,satir,renk)
	if ss > 0 then
			for kk=1 to ss
     	  Sh1.Cells(satir,kk+10).interior.colorindex=renk
			next
		end if
End Sub

Sub gunyaz(sh1,sure)
		kolonN=10
		for t=1 to sure
			sh1.Cells(3,kolonN+t).value=t&"gün"
		next
End Sub
